# MatchEngine assumptions register

Authoritative, persistent checklist. Base: merged PR194 `e828f5a1e41bbec2101c71f42739037702276cce`. PR195 audits and builds measurement infrastructure; canonical **NOT READY**, **B remains default**, independent confirmation **sealed**. This is a source-backed initial register, not proof that every behaviour is understood.

## Reading and updating

Each item separates implementation correctness (I), physical feasibility (F), empirical calibration (E), tactical validity (T), full-match transfer (M), and independent confirmation (C). A related test is partial I/F evidence only. `partial` never means the entire item passed. No item is closed by a source edit or a green CI run.

At each subsequent MatchEngine PR: read this register; name exact IDs; freeze source/evidence and hypotheses; run baseline laboratories; make scoped changes; repeat paired development tests; record regressions/uncertainty; update statuses and link the PR plus commit and reproducible closure evidence; leave other items open. Never delete or renumber retired items: retain their history and replacement IDs.

Allowed statuses: `[ ] NOT_AUDITED`, `[ ] AUDITED_UNVALIDATED`, `[ ] LAB_REQUIRED`, `[ ] LAB_ACTIVE`, `[ ] CALIBRATION_REQUIRED`, `[ ] REFACTOR_REQUIRED`, `[ ] BLOCKED`, `[ ] DEVELOPMENT_VALIDATED`, `[ ] INDEPENDENT_VALIDATION_REQUIRED`, `[x] COMPLETE`. Only COMPLETE checks the box.

Priority: P0 shared foundation/invalid decision risk; P1 major match consequence; P2 local limitation/missing capability; P3 uncommon/future polish. Priorities indicate plausible causal impact, not proven full-match effect size.

[Coverage and exclusions](SOURCE_AUDIT.md), [prior evidence](PRIOR_RESEARCH.md), [calibration protocol](CALIBRATION_PLAN.md), [dependency map](MECHANISM_DEPENDENCIES.md).

## Decision architecture and risk

### DEC-001 — Audit whether all 0–1 risk values represent actual probabilities or only heuristic scores.

- [ ] LAB_REQUIRED
- **Category / subsystem:** Decision architecture and risk.
- **Classification / priority / severity:** `PROBABILITY_CLAIM`; **P0**; foundational, multiple downstream decisions/outcomes.
- **Implementation:** [`boundedDecision.constructBoundedCandidates`](../../src/core/matchSimulation/boundedDecision.ts#L122); [`decisionStateValue.evaluateStateValueAction`](../../src/core/matchSimulation/decisionStateValue.ts#L206).
- **Current values / structural rule:** risk is clamped to 0–1; branch probabilities sum to one, without a fitted event model.
- **Origin / provenance:** related research [PR176](../PR176_MATCH_ENGINE_MATHEMATICAL_AUDIT.md), [PR177](../PR177_MATCH_ENGINE_REFORM_IMPLEMENTATION.md), [PR178](../PR178_CONTACT_PHYSICS_AND_MATCH_REFORM_VALIDATION.md), [PR179](../PR179_ROLE_DRIVEN_FOOTBALL_INTELLIGENCE.md), [PR182](../PR182_TACTICAL_INTELLIGENCE_AND_POLICY_VALIDATION.md), [PR186](../PR186_TACTICAL_COGNITION_AND_ATTRIBUTE_DIAGNOSTICS.md), [PR187](../PR187_BOUNDED_FOOTBALL_INTELLIGENCE_AND_POLICY_VALIDATION.md), [PR188](../PR188_RELATIVE_DECISION_MAKING_AND_MATCH_STABILIZATION.md), [PR193](../PR193_TRANSITION_INTELLIGENCE_STRATEGIC_CLEARANCES_AND_OPPORTUNITY_WINDOWS.md), [PR194](../PR194_ACTIVE_PERCEPTION_PRE_RECEPTION_INTELLIGENCE_AND_TEAM_TEMPO.md); current file last-touch commits `boundedDecision` b8fdb0a8, `decisionStateValue` 7778b36f. Last touch is not claimed as the coefficient's original introduction; exact initial numeric provenance remains unestablished unless the linked report states it.
- **Existing evidence:** source trace above; direct importing tests [boundedIntelligence.test.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/boundedIntelligence.test.ts), [pr188RelativeDecision.test.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/pr188RelativeDecision.test.ts), [pr193Transition.test.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/pr193Transition.test.ts). Historical report evidence retains its original source and scope; PR195 pilot evidence is development-only.
- **Validation layers:** I: partial source/test contracts; F: partial canonical geometry/execution, not full mechanism validation; E: uncalibrated; T: unvalidated item-specific choice; M: prior development findings are mixed/negative, no isolated transfer claim; C: sealed/unrun.
- **Missing evidence:** No item-specific empirical calibration with uncertainty, no isolated full-match causal transfer, and no independent confirmation.
- **Hypothesised consequences / expected impact:** normalised scores can look trustworthy while discarding useful actions. This is a hypothesis unless the source rule itself demonstrates the limitation.
- **Dependencies / affected policies:** `PASS-001`, `SHOT-001`, `DEF-001`, `TACT-009`. legacy/risk-ablation, B/R1/R2/R3, bounded O/H/J; shared canonical executor. Actual importing consumers: [assumptionCalibration.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/assumptionCalibration.ts), [boundedIntelligence.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/boundedIntelligence.ts), [preReceptionPlanning.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/preReceptionPlanning.ts), [transitionLaboratory.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/transitionLaboratory.ts), [decisionPolicy.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/decisionPolicy.ts).
- **Proposed validation laboratory:** A/B/C reliability by event and policy.
- **Calibration / replacement strategy:** freeze this rule and its event/information contract; distinguish prediction error from contact/execution and selection error; measure the named lab before changing coefficients. Fit or replace only the causally supported component; keep subjective belief separate from engineering truth.
- **Acceptance conditions:** A/B/C reliability by event and policy runs reproducibly with positive and negative controls, exact source/input/seed/episode identity, rejection/censor counts and skill/geometry strata; intended effect and regressions are measured against the frozen rule. Required I/F/E/T/M/C layers must each have explicit evidence or a justified remaining gate. No completion based on aggregate event targets.
- **Status rationale / blockers:** source-audited; item-specific laboratory still required.
- **Closure PR / commit / evidence:** none; **OPEN**. Related historical PRs are not closure evidence for this item.

### DEC-006 — Audit the value assigned to continued possession versus the available alternatives.

- [ ] LAB_REQUIRED
- **Category / subsystem:** Decision architecture and risk.
- **Classification / priority / severity:** `HEURISTIC_UTILITY`; **P0**; foundational, multiple downstream decisions/outcomes.

### PHYS-001 — Physical gravity and law-defined goal dimensions versus empirical response parameters.

## Dynamic initiative and cover

### TACT-013 — Dynamic attacking initiative from nominal defensive positions and cover-aware advancement.

- [ ] LAB_ACTIVE
- **Category / subsystem:** Tactical initiative and on-ball carry choice.
- **Classification / priority / severity:** `TACTICAL_ASSUMPTION`; **P1**; forward opportunity and exposed counterattack consequences.
- **Implementation:** [receivingPositions.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/receivingPositions.ts), [boundedDecision.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/boundedDecision.ts), [matchActions.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/matchActions.ts), [disruptionLedger.ts](../../src/core/matchSimulation/disruptionLedger.ts).
- **Current rule / discovery:** off-ball receiving jobs protect defend duties and centre-backs; native on-ball menus already contain ordinary carries. This is a positional tendency and value/information question, not a universal physical prohibition. No player identity or defender bonus is introduced.
- **Origin / provenance:** PR198 source audit; existing TACT-003 covers shape displacement, TACT-006 covers territory/cover, DRIB-002/003 cover routes/control. This dedicated item records the previously implicit role/initiative comparison without duplicating their physical calibration.
- **Existing evidence:** [paired defender-carry laboratory](../performance/pr198/disruption-v3.json.gz), native bounded/full-world menus, actual actor paths, challenges, loose intervals, losses and cover.
- **Validation layers:** I: witnessed menu/role contracts; F: ordinary carry/duel physics with positive and negative continuations; E: uncalibrated; T: opportunity/cover-aware role choice not validated; M: only shared experimental development pairs, no isolated role-calibration claim; C: sealed/unrun.
- **Dependencies / affected policies:** TACT-001/003/006/009/010, DRIB-002/003, DEC-003/005/007/008, DEF-010; all on-ball policies.
- **Proposed laboratory / acceptance:** paired identical states with open corridor, good challenge, teammate cover and exposed counterattack; vary own technique/reading/composure, observed pressure, coach role, risk tolerance and match context. Require measured useful progress and retained dangerous failures, comparable alternatives, full-match transfer and all six evidence layers before closure.
- **Status rationale / remaining gates:** diagnoses existing capability and role/value limits; no general tactical liberalisation or new utility bonus. Independent state value and role-aware calibration remain future work.
- **Closure PR / commit / evidence:** PR198 archived source fingerprints and [report](../PR198_RECEIVER_SYNCHRONISATION_AUTONOMOUS_OPPORTUNITIES_AND_EMERGENT_DISRUPTION.md); no closure commit. **OPEN.**
